# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

#if BEAM_DEBUG_INFERENCE_TRACE
std::vector<half> stream1_reference_linear(
    const std::vector<half>& input,
    const half* weight,
    std::uint32_t input_cols,
    std::uint32_t output_cols) {
    std::vector<half> output(output_cols);
    for (std::uint32_t out = 0; out < output_cols; ++out) {
        float acc = 0.0f;
        for (std::uint32_t in = 0; in < input_cols; ++in) {
            acc += __half2float(input[in]) * __half2float(weight[in * output_cols + out]);
        }
        output[out] = __float2half(acc);
    }
    return output;
}

void stream1_reference_bias_relu(std::vector<half>& values, const half* bias) {
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < values.size(); ++i) {
        const float x = __half2float(values[i]) + __half2float(bias[i]);
        values[i] = __float2half(x > 0.0f ? x : 0.0f);
    }
}

void stream1_reference_residual_add_bias_relu(
    std::vector<half>& values,
    const std::vector<half>& residual,
    const half* bias) {
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < values.size(); ++i) {
        const float x = __half2float(values[i]) + __half2float(residual[i]) + __half2float(bias[i]);
        values[i] = __float2half(x > 0.0f ? x : 0.0f);
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Explicit Stream1 piece-transformer backend launcher.

This is a thin registry for the three backend families we keep developing:

- pytorch: Python Torch over exported Stream1 weights.
- libtorch: explicit C++ LibTorch tool, built only with BEAM_ENABLE_LIBTORCH_STREAM1=ON.
- native_cutlass: native CUDA/CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path via stream_benchmark.

The registry is intentionally not a fallback chain. A caller selects exactly one
backend and mode; missing tools or dependencies should fail at that backend.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Mapping, Sequence


REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

    owner: str
    modes: tuple[str, ...]
    default_mode: str
    description: str

@dataclass(frozen=True)

    mode: str
    command: tuple[str, ...]
    env: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()

    def as_dict(self) -> Dict[str, object]:

            "backend": self.backend,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "command": list(self.command),
            "env": dict(self.env),
        }

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Plain PyTorch Stream1 piece-transformer inference benchmark.

This intentionally benchmarks the exported Stream1 weights as a PyTorch model.
It is not a runtime fallback path.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F


SCORE_MAX_Q = 300.0
SCORE_SCALE = 1024.0
FNV64_OFFSET = 1469598103934665603
FNV64_PRIME = 1099511628211
FNV64_MASK = (1 << 64) - 1


def parse_positive_ints(text: str, label: str) -> List[int]:
    values = []
    for part in text.split(","):
        part = part.strip()
        if part:
            value = int(part)
            if value <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{label} values must be positive")
            values.append(value)
    if not values:
        raise ValueError(f"at least one {label} value is required")
    return values


def parse_batch_sizes(text: str) -> List[int]:
    return parse_positive_ints(text, "batch size")


def product(shape: Iterable[int]) -> int:
    n = 1
    for value in shape:
        n *= int(value)
    return n


def load_file_tensor(path: Path, shape: Tuple[int, ...], dtype: torch.dtype, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
    expected = product(shape)
    tensor = torch.from_file(str(path), shared=False, size=expected, dtype=dtype)
    i<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3315" />er_launch_group * iters) / (elapsed_ms / 1000.0)
    candidates_per_s = parents_per_s * model.move_count
    flops_per_parent = model.estimated_flops_per_parent()
    achieved_tflops = parents_per_s * flops_per_parent / 1.0e12
    peak_gib = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated(model.device) / (1024.0**3)
    return {
        "batch": batch,
        "concurrency": concurrency,
        "rows_per_launch_group": rows_per_launch_group,
        "iters": iters,
        "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
        "ms_per_iter": elapsed_ms / iters,
        "parents_per_s": parents_per_s,
        "candidates_per_s": candidates_per_s,
        "estimated_flops_per_parent": flops_per_parent,
        "achieved_tflops": achieved_tflops,
        "peak_mem_gib": peak_gib,
        "checksum": checksum,
        "score_key_digest": score_digest,
        "first_score_keys": first_score_keys,
        "status": "ok",
    }


def write_report(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, object]], metadata: Dict[str, object]) -> None:
    best = max((row for row in rows if row.get("status") == "ok"), key=lambda r: float(r["candidates_per_s"]), default=None)

#!/bin/bash
#SBATCH --partition=kaf12
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:8

test "$(git -C "$SOURCE" rev-parse --short=7 HEAD)" = 1b0d40c

export CUDA_DEVICE_ORDER=PCI_BUS_ID
export NCCL_DEBUG=INFO
export NCCL_ASYNC_ERROR_HANDLING=1

  -DMGT_ENABLE_CUDA=ON \
  -DMGT_ENABLE_NCCL=ON \

  --target test_a100_bf16_runtime

ctest --test-dir "$BUILD" \
  -R '^a100_bf16_runtime$' \
  --output-on-failure --no-tests=error

export MGT_BN_ID="$ROOT/nccl-bn-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_WEIGHT_ID="$ROOT/nccl-weight-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_METRICS_ID="$ROOT/nccl-metrics-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"

rm -f "$MGT_BN_ID" "$MGT_WEIGHT_ID" "$MGT_METRICS_ID"

srun --ntasks=8 --cpus-per-task=4 --kill-on-bad-exit=1 \
  "$BUILD/test_a100_bf16_runtime"

echo A100_THREE_NCCL_CONTEXTS_V8_OK
BASH

jid=$(sbatch --parsable -p kaf12 runtime-nccl-v8.sbatch)
echo "JOB_ID=$jid"
echo "OUTPUT=$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

while squeue -h -j "$jid" | grep -q .; do
  squeue -j "$jid"
  sleep 20
done

sacct -j "$jid" --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,NodeList
cat "$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

- Порядок 24 move names в наших файлах совпадает, но это ещё не доказывает соответствие выходных нейронов исходной модели.
- Наш `INFERENCE_PARITY_OK` недостаточен: тестирует синтетические состояния, только первую строку и допускает расхождение score до `3072`. Он не проверяет порядок ходов, `argmin` и top-k.

Следующая правильная проверка: взять реальные состояния вдоль известного решения p1000 и для каждого состояния вывести все 24 оценки одновременно из:

1. Kaggle-эквивалентного LibTorch;
2. PyTorch;
3. нашего native eager;
4. нашего native graph.

Сравнить нужно таблицей `move → raw logit → score_key → rank`, особенно на глубине 6, где эталонный путь исчезает. Это сразу разделит два варианта:

- LibTorch/PyTorch выбирают правильный ход, native — другой: сломан aligned/native inference.
- Все backend дают одинаковый порядок: модель действительно плохо ранжирует этот ход, а проблема уже в ширине/отсечении beam.

То есть пока утверждать «модель ошибается» нельзя. Сначала нужен именно real-state rank parity; текущий parity-gate этого не проверяет.

#if BEAM_DEBUG_INFERENCE_TRACE
std::vector<half> stream1_reference_linear(
    const std::vector<half>& input,
    const half* weight,
    std::uint32_t input_cols,
    std::uint32_t output_cols) {
    std::vector<half> output(output_cols);
    for (std::uint32_t out = 0; out < output_cols; ++out) {
        float acc = 0.0f;
        for (std::uint32_t in = 0; in < input_cols; ++in) {
            acc += __half2float(input[in]) * __half2float(weight[in * output_cols + out]);
        }
        output[out] = __float2half(acc);
    }
    return output;
}

void stream1_reference_bias_relu(std::vector<half>& values, const half* bias) {
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < values.size(); ++i) {
        const float x = __half2float(values[i]) + __half2float(bias[i]);
        values[i] = __float2half(x > 0.0f ? x : 0.0f);
    }
}

void stream1_reference_residual_add_bias_relu(
    std::vector<half>& values,
    const std::vector<half>& residual,
    const half* bias) {
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < values.size(); ++i) {
        const float x = __half2float(values[i]) + __half2float(residual[i]) + __half2float(bias[i]);
        values[i] = __float2half(x > 0.0f ? x : 0.0f);
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Explicit Stream1 piece-transformer backend launcher.

This is a thin registry for the three backend families we keep developing:

- pytorch: Python Torch over exported Stream1 weights.
- libtorch: explicit C++ LibTorch tool, built only with BEAM_ENABLE_LIBTORCH_STREAM1=ON.
- native_cutlass: native CUDA/CUTLASS Stream1 transformer path via stream_benchmark.

The registry is intentionally not a fallback chain. A caller selects exactly one
backend and mode; missing tools or dependencies should fail at that backend.
"""

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Plain PyTorch Stream1 piece-transformer inference benchmark.

This intentionally benchmarks the exported Stream1 weights as a PyTorch model.
It is not a runtime fallback path.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple

import torch

FNV64_PRIME = 1099511628211
FNV64_MASK = (1 << 64) - 1


def parse_positive_ints(text: str, label: str) -> List[int]:
    values = []
    for part in text.split(","):
        part = part.strip()
        if part:
            value = int(part)
            if value <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{label} values must be positive")
            values.append(value)
    if not values:
        raise ValueError(f"at least one {label} value is required")
    return values


def parse_batch_sizes(text: str) -> List[int]:
    return parse_positive_ints(text, "batch size")


def product(shape: Iterable[int]) -> int:
    n = 1
    for value in shape:
        n *= int(value)
    return n


def load_file_tensor(path: Path, shape: Tuple[int, ...], dtype: torch.dtype, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
    expected = product(shape)
    tensor = torch.from_file(str(path), shared=False, size=expected, dtype=dtype)

using namespace beam;

#!/bin/bash
#SBATCH --partition=kaf12
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:8
#SBATCH --mem=128G
#SBATCH --time=00:15:00

NCCL_ROOT=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/ninja-venv/lib/python3.13/site-packages/nvidia/nccl

test "$(git -C "$SOURCE" rev-parse --short=7 HEAD)" = 1b0d40c

export CUDA_DEVICE_ORDER=PCI_BUS_ID
export NCCL_DEBUG=INFO
export NCCL_ASYNC_ERROR_HANDLING=1

  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DMGT_ENABLE_CUDA=ON \
  -DMGT_ENABLE_NCCL=ON \

ctest --test-dir "$BUILD" \
  -R '^a100_bf16_runtime$' \
  --output-on-failure --no-tests=error

export MGT_BN_ID="$ROOT/nccl-bn-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_WEIGHT_ID="$ROOT/nccl-weight-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_METRICS_ID="$ROOT/nccl-metrics-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"

srun --ntasks=8 --cpus-per-task=4 --kill-on-bad-exit=1 \

echo A100_THREE_NCCL_CONTEXTS_V8_OK

echo "OUTPUT=$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

while squeue -h -j "$jid" | grep -q .; do
  squeue -j "$jid"
  sleep 20
done

sacct -j "$jid" --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,NodeList
cat "$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

[248] assistant: Изучил. Kaggle чинить в части global top‑K не нужно: там уже тот же строгий глобальный top‑K, что и на кластере.

Подтверждённые различия:

- Kaggle закреплён на commit `a1db0e6`.
- Кластер работал на `d319f6d`.
- Между ними код threshold, Stream 3/4 selection и final exchange не менялся.
- Изменился именно Transformer-инференс:
  - `a1db0e6`: старый специализированный путь;
  - `d319f6d`: aligned/masked sequence, для Cube4 последовательность дополняется до alignment и исполняется другим FMHA layout.
- Kaggle на T4 использует FP16.
- Кластер на A100 использует BF16.
- Checkpoint одинаковый, но вычислительный граф и численная арифметика не одинаковы.

Поэтому оба запуска честно строят глобальный top‑K, но по разным наборам численных оценок. Даже небольшое изменение score меняет frontier, затем различие лавинообразно накапливается. К depth 6 на кластере уже существуют другие 60M состояний, и ветка со score `18398` оказывается хуже threshold `17736`.

То есть предыдущая версия про shard-local квоты была неправильной. Настоящая причина:

`старый FP16 Transformer на T4 ≠ новый aligned BF16 Transformer на A100`.

Дополнительно подтверждено:

- native A100: `18398`;
- PyTorch/BF16-проверка: `18400`;
- значит новый A100 inference корректен относительно своей BF16 reference;
- это ничего не говорит о совпадении с Kaggle FP16/старым kernel.

Если обновить Kaggle до `d319f6d`, global top‑K останется таким же, но найденное раньше решение может как раз исчезнуть. Для строгого сравнения нужно запустить на Kaggle трассировку того же пути на `d319f6d` и сравнить depth 0–6 с кластером. Никакую политику top‑K сейчас менять не следует.

[250] assistant: Да. По факту Kaggle решил puzzle 1000 с существенно меньшим лучом, поэтому объяснение «из-за BF16 правильная ветка немного переставилась» само по себе недостаточно. При 60M модель должна иметь большой запас, а при 820M — тем более.

Сейчас доказано только:

- один конкретный Kaggle-путь на A100/BF16 отсекается на depth 6;
- score native совпадает с PyTorch/BF16;
- global top‑K работает по контракту.

Но это не доказывает исправность всего поиска: beam обязан найти хотя бы другой путь. Значит надо воспроизвести Kaggle конфигурацию на кластере без одновременной смены пяти факторов:

- commit `a1db0e6`;
- FP16;
- `WORLD_SIZE=2`;
- `SHARD_COUNT=4`;
- тот же фактический beam;
- BFS radius 4;
- puzzle 1000.

Если такой запуск решает — затем по одному меняем commit, dtype и world size и находим конкретный фактор. Если не решает, значит различается ещё checkpoint export, вход или Kaggle-результат.

И да: production на 820M нельзя считать исправным только потому, что traced путь при 60M был отрезан. Отсутствие решения при 820M остаётся отдельным серьёзным симптомом.

```bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444-fp16-parity
REPO="$ROOT/repo"
MODEL="$ROOT/model"
DATA=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444a100/cayley-py-444-cube
PIN=a1db0e6d9bb5458c8a842b37dfa99572d3025667

mkdir -p "$ROOT" "$MODEL"

if [ ! -f "$ROOT/model.zip" ]; then
  curl -fL \
    https://www.kaggle.com/api/v1/datasets/download/trydotatwo/cube4-full-transformer-inference \
    -o "$ROOT/model.zip"
fi

if [ ! -f "$MODEL/.ready" ]; then
  unzip -q -o "$ROOT/model.zip" -d "$MODEL"
  touch "$MODEL/.ready"
fi

if [ ! -d "$REPO/.git" ]; then
  git clone https://github.com/TryDotAtwo/MultiGPUBeamSearch.git "$REPO"
fi

git -C "$REPO" fetch origin "$PIN"
git -C "$REPO" checkout --detach FETCH_HEAD
test "$(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$PIN"

test -f "$DATA/puzzle_info.json"
test -f "$DATA/test.csv"

cat > "$ROOT/parity.sbatch" <<'BASH'
#!/bin/bash
#SBATCH --partition=kaf12
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks=1
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=16
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:2
#SBATCH --mem=256G
#SBATCH --time=24:00:00
#SBATCH --output=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444-fp16-parity/parity-%j.out

set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444-fp16-parity
REPO="$ROOT/repo"
MODEL="$ROOT/model"
DATA=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444a100/cayley-py-444-cube
PY=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/ninja-venv/bin/python
PIN=a1db0e6d9bb5458c8a842b37dfa99572d3025667

test "$(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$PIN"

CHECKPOINT=$(find "$MODEL" -type f -name model.pth -print -quit)
METADATA=$(find "$MODEL" -type f -name model.json -print -quit)
GENERATORS=$(find "$MODEL" -type f -name p002.json -print -quit)

test -n "$CHECKPOINT"
test -n "$METADATA"
test -n "$GENERATORS"

rm -rf "$REPO/stream1_weights"
"$PY" "$REPO/tools/export_stream1_transformer.py" \
  --weights "$CHECKPOINT" \
  --out "$REPO/stream1_weights" \
  --dtype fp16 \
  --num-classes 6 \
  --metadata "$METADATA" \
  --generators "$GENERATORS" \
  --source-root "$MODEL"

mkdir -p "$REPO/data"
cp "$DATA/puzzle_info.json" "$REPO/data/puzzle_info.json"
cp "$DATA/test.csv" "$REPO/data/test.csv"

export JOB_DIR="$ROOT"
export REPO_DIR="$REPO"
export BUILD_DIR="$ROOT/build-a100-fp16"
export CUTLASS_DIR=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/cutlass
export NINJA_VENV_DIR=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/ninja-venv

export PUZZLE_ID=1000
export DEPTH_LIMIT=50
export BEAM_WIDTH=60000000

export TORCHRUN_NNODES=1
export TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE=2
export TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK=0
export TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT=127.0.0.1:29610

export BEAM_B_MICRO=8192
export BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY=4
export BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS=4
export SHARD_COUNT=4
export SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM=1250000
export STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES=196608
export STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES=196608

export BEAM_WEIGHT_DIR="$REPO/stream1_weights"
export BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH="$DATA/puzzle_info.json"
export BEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON="$DATA/puzzle_info.json"
export BEAM_TEST_CSV="$DATA/test.csv"

export BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS=4
export BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_MAX_ENTRIES=3000000
export BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=static_hybrid
export BEAM_HISTORY_RAM_BYTES=$((64 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024))
export BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_BYTES=$((1024 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024))

BASH

cd "$ROOT"
jid=$(sbatch --parsable parity.sbatch)

echo "JOB_ID=$jid"
echo "OUTPUT=$ROOT/parity-$jid.out"
echo "LIVE_LOG=tail -n 100 -f $ROOT/parity-$jid.out"

while squeue -h -j "$jid" | grep -q .; do
  squeue -j "$jid"
  sleep 20
done

sacct -j "$jid" --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,NodeList
tail -n 300 "$ROOT/parity-$jid.out"

[248] assistant: Изучил. Kaggle чинить в части global top‑K не нужно: там уже тот же строгий глобальный top‑K, что и на кластере.

Подтверждённые различия:

- Kaggle закреплён на commit `a1db0e6`.
- Кластер работал на `d319f6d`.
- Между ними код threshold, Stream 3/4 selection и final exchange не менялся.
- Изменился именно Transformer-инференс:
  - `a1db0e6`: старый специализированный путь;
  - `d319f6d`: aligned/masked sequence, для Cube4 последовательность дополняется до alignment и исполняется другим FMHA layout.
- Kaggle на T4 использует FP16.
- Кластер на A100 использует BF16.
- Checkpoint одинаковый, но вычислительный граф и численная арифметика не одинаковы.

Поэтому оба запуска честно строят глобальный top‑K, но по разным наборам численных оценок. Даже небольшое изменение score меняет frontier, затем различие лавинообразно накапливается. К depth 6 на кластере уже существуют другие 60M состояний, и ветка со score `18398` оказывается хуже threshold `17736`.

То есть предыдущая версия про shard-local квоты была неправильной. Настоящая причина:

`старый FP16 Transformer на T4 ≠ новый aligned BF16 Transformer на A100`.

Дополнительно подтверждено:

- native A100: `18398`;
- PyTorch/BF16-проверка: `18400`;
- значит новый A100 inference корректен относительно своей BF16 reference;
- это ничего не говорит о совпадении с Kaggle FP16/старым kernel.

Если обновить Kaggle до `d319f6d`, global top‑K останется таким же, но найденное раньше решение может как раз исчезнуть. Для строгого сравнения нужно запустить на Kaggle трассировку того же пути на `d319f6d` и сравнить depth 0–6 с кластером. Никакую политику top‑K сейчас менять не следует.

[250] assistant: Да. По факту Kaggle решил puzzle 1000 с существенно меньшим лучом, поэтому объяснение «из-за BF16 правильная ветка немного переставилась» само по себе недостаточно. При 60M модель должна иметь большой запас, а при 820M — тем более.

Сейчас доказано только:

- один конкретный Kaggle-путь на A100/BF16 отсекается на depth 6;
- score native совпадает с PyTorch/BF16;
- global top‑K работает по контракту.

Но это не доказывает исправность всего поиска: beam обязан найти хотя бы другой путь. Значит надо воспроизвести Kaggle конфигурацию на кластере без одновременной смены пяти факторов:

- commit `a1db0e6`;
- FP16;
- `WORLD_SIZE=2`;
- `SHARD_COUNT=4`;
- тот же фактический beam;
- BFS radius 4;
- puzzle 1000.

Если такой запуск решает — затем по одному меняем commit, dtype и world size и находим конкретный фактор. Если не решает, значит различается ещё checkpoint export, вход или Kaggle-результат.

И да: production на 820M нельзя считать исправным только потому, что traced путь при 60M был отрезан. Отсутствие решения при 820M остаётся отдельным серьёзным симптомом.

Текущий Kaggle notebook сейчас ещё `RUNNING`, поэтому его случайно выбранный beam пока неизвестен. Для решающего теста фиксируем более сильный вариант: 60M, старый Kaggle commit, FP16, 2 GPU, shard 4, BFS 4.

```bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444-fp16-parity
REPO="$ROOT/repo"
MODEL="$ROOT/model"
DATA=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444a100/cayley-py-444-cube
PIN=a1db0e6d9bb5458c8a842b37dfa99572d3025667

mkdir -p "$ROOT" "$MODEL"

if [ ! -f "$ROOT/model.zip" ]; then
  curl -fL \
    https://www.kaggle.com/api/v1/datasets/download/trydotatwo/cube4-full-transformer-inference \
    -o "$ROOT/model.zip"
fi

if [ ! -f "$MODEL/.ready" ]; then
  unzip -q -o "$ROOT/model.zip" -d "$MODEL"
  touch "$MODEL/.ready"
fi

if [ ! -d "$REPO/.git" ]; then
  git clone https://github.com/TryDotAtwo/MultiGPUBeamSearch.git "$REPO"
fi

git -C "$REPO" fetch origin "$PIN"
git -C "$REPO" checkout --detach FETCH_HEAD
test "$(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$PIN"

test -f "$DATA/puzzle_info.json"
test -f "$DATA/test.csv"

cat > "$ROOT/parity.sbatch" <<'BASH'
#!/bin/bash
#SBATCH --partition=kaf12
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks=1
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=16
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:2
#SBATCH --mem=256G
#SBATCH --time=24:00:00
#SBATCH --output=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444-fp16-parity/parity-%j.out

set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444-fp16-parity
REPO="$ROOT/repo"
MODEL="$ROOT/model"
DATA=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam444a100/cayley-py-444-cube
PY=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/ninja-venv/bin/python
PIN=a1db0e6d9bb5458c8a842b37dfa99572d3025667

test "$(git -C "$REPO" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$PIN"

CHECKPOINT=$(find "$MODEL" -type f -name model.pth -print -quit)
METADATA=$(find "$MODEL" -type f -name model.json -print -quit)
GENERATORS=$(find "$MODEL" -type f -name p002.json -print -quit)

test -n "$CHECKPOINT"
test -n "$METADATA"
test -n "$GENERATORS"

rm -rf "$REPO/stream1_weights"
"$PY" "$REPO/tools/export_stream1_transformer.py" \
  --weights "$CHECKPOINT" \
  --out "$REPO/stream1_weights" \
  --dtype fp16 \
  --num-classes 6 \
  --metadata "$METADATA" \
  --generators "$GENERATORS" \
  --source-root "$MODEL"

mkdir -p "$REPO/data"
cp "$DATA/puzzle_info.json" "$REPO/data/puzzle_info.json"
cp "$DATA/test.csv" "$REPO/data/test.csv"

export JOB_DIR="$ROOT"
export REPO_DIR="$REPO"
export BUILD_DIR="$ROOT/build-a100-fp16"
export CUTLASS_DIR=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/cutlass
export NINJA_VENV_DIR=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/ninja-venv

export PUZZLE_ID=1000
export DEPTH_LIMIT=50
export BEAM_WIDTH=60000000

export TORCHRUN_NNODES=1
export TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE=2
export TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK=0
export TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT=127.0.0.1:29610

export BEAM_B_MICRO=8192
export BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY=4
export BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS=4
export SHARD_COUNT=4
export SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM=1250000
export STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES=196608
export STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES=196608

export BEAM_WEIGHT_DIR="$REPO/stream1_weights"
export BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH="$DATA/puzzle_info.json"
export BEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON="$DATA/puzzle_info.json"
export BEAM_TEST_CSV="$DATA/test.csv"

export BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS=4
export BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_MAX_ENTRIES=3000000
export BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=static_hybrid
export BEAM_HISTORY_RAM_BYTES=$((64 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024))
export BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_BYTES=$((1024 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024))
export BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH="$ROOT/history"
export BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_EXCHANGE_SCALE_PPM=2000000

bash "$REPO/hpc/start_8xa100_beamsearch.sh"
BASH

cd "$ROOT"
jid=$(sbatch --parsable parity.sbatch)

echo "JOB_ID=$jid"
echo "OUTPUT=$ROOT/parity-$jid.out"
echo "LIVE_LOG=tail -n 100 -f $ROOT/parity-$jid.out"

while squeue -h -j "$jid" | grep -q .; do
  squeue -j "$jid"
  sleep 20
done

sacct -j "$jid" --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,NodeList
tail -n 300 "$ROOT/parity-$jid.out"
```

#!/bin/bash

#SBATCH --gres=gpu:8

cmake --build "$BUILD" --parallel 32 \
  --target test_a100_bf16_runtime

ctest --test-dir "$BUILD" \
  -R '^a100_bf16_runtime$' \
  --output-on-failure --no-tests=error

export MGT_BN_ID="$ROOT/nccl-bn-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_WEIGHT_ID="$ROOT/nccl-weight-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_METRICS_ID="$ROOT/nccl-metrics-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"

rm -f "$MGT_BN_ID" "$MGT_WEIGHT_ID" "$MGT_METRICS_ID"

srun --ntasks=8 --cpus-per-task=4 --kill-on-bad-exit=1 \

echo A100_THREE_NCCL_CONTEXTS_V8_OK
BASH

jid=$(sbatch --parsable -p kaf12 runtime-nccl-v8.sbatch)

echo "OUTPUT=$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

while squeue -h -j "$jid" | grep -q .; do
  squeue -j "$jid"
  sleep 20
done

sacct -j "$jid" --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,NodeList
cat "$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

 # Changelog
 
+- Published the validated CayleyPy results-repository control plane to
+  `TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results` main via PR #1. The public repository now
+  has append-only schema/replay validation, deterministic indexes, serialized
+  staging promotion, and an exact-candidate Check Run contract. Main branch
+  protection requires `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` from the GitHub
+  Actions App (`app_id=15368`), strict up-to-date PRs, and disallows force
+  pushes/deletion. Cloudflare ingest remains disabled until authenticated
+  staging resources, GitHub App secrets, and the 100-publisher recovery gate
+  pass; no beam-search/CUDA architecture was changed.
+
+## 2026-07-22
+- Updated the IHES GitHub-release preparation path to use
+  `p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth` as the default model release asset for
+  `hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`.
+- Added a `model.pth.release_asset` marker check so the default cluster
+  `model.pth` is re-downloaded and Stream1 weights are re-exported when the
+  configured release asset changes, preventing a stale old checkpoint from
+  being reused silently.
+
+## 2026-06-29
+- Added a separate Stream1 transformer export path for Kaggle
+  `piece_transformer` checkpoints. `tools/export_stream1_transformer.py`
+  detects model dimensions from the checkpoint/metadata/generator files,
+  exports the p900 fast input form (`fast_slot_projected` and
+  `fast_piece_static`), transformer block weights, LayerNorm parameters, and a
+  `backend=piece_transformer` manifest without changing the existing MLP
+  exporter.

#!/bin/bash
#SBATCH --partition=kaf12
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --gres=gpu:8
#SBATCH --mem=128G
#SBATCH --time=00:15:00
#SBATCH --output=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/a100-prepared-gate/runtime-nccl-v8-%j.out

set -euo pipefail

ROOT=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/a100-prepared-gate
SOURCE="$ROOT/source"
BUILD="$ROOT/build-nccl-v8"
NCCL_ROOT=/mnt/pool/3/vokirova/ninja-venv/lib/python3.13/site-packages/nvidia/nccl

test "$(git -C "$SOURCE" rev-parse --short=7 HEAD)" = 1b0d40c

export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="$NCCL_ROOT/lib:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}"
export CUDA_DEVICE_ORDER=PCI_BUS_ID
export NCCL_DEBUG=INFO
export NCCL_ASYNC_ERROR_HANDLING=1

cmake -S "$SOURCE/native" -B "$BUILD" \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DMGT_ENABLE_CUDA=ON \
  -DMGT_ENABLE_NCCL=ON \
  -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=80 \
  -DMGT_AUTO_CUTLASS_HALF_GEMM=OFF \
  -DNCCL_INCLUDE_DIR:PATH="$NCCL_ROOT/include" \
  -DNCCL_LIB:FILEPATH="$NCCL_ROOT/lib/libnccl.so.2"

cmake --build "$BUILD" --parallel 32 \
  --target test_a100_bf16_runtime

ctest --test-dir "$BUILD" \
  -R '^a100_bf16_runtime$' \
  --output-on-failure --no-tests=error

export MGT_BN_ID="$ROOT/nccl-bn-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_WEIGHT_ID="$ROOT/nccl-weight-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"
export MGT_METRICS_ID="$ROOT/nccl-metrics-${SLURM_JOB_ID}.id"

rm -f "$MGT_BN_ID" "$MGT_WEIGHT_ID" "$MGT_METRICS_ID"

srun --ntasks=8 --cpus-per-task=4 --kill-on-bad-exit=1 \
  "$BUILD/test_a100_bf16_runtime"

echo A100_THREE_NCCL_CONTEXTS_V8_OK
BASH

jid=$(sbatch --parsable -p kaf12 runtime-nccl-v8.sbatch)
echo "JOB_ID=$jid"
echo "OUTPUT=$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

while squeue -h -j "$jid" | grep -q .; do
  squeue -j "$jid"
  sleep 20
done

sacct -j "$jid" --format=JobID,State,ExitCode,Elapsed,NodeList
cat "$ROOT/runtime-nccl-v8-$jid.out"

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

See test_results/kaggle_tetraminx_multigpu_2026-07-24.md.
- Added the design contract for a universal private Kaggle 2xT4 MLP notebook
  with independently measured output-1/output-move-count profiles at beam
  anchors `2**16..2**25`. The design preserves the user's exact beam, selects
  runtime parameters by nearest rounded-log2 anchor, and documents the two
  supported checkpoint schemas explicitly.
- Diagnosed integrated Tetraminx Kaggle v1 after completed training/export/build. Both T4 ranks failed the manual layout guard because the `2**21` beam-derived shard capacity was below the output-1 Stream3 batch. Updated only notebook configuration to clamp shard capacity to `196608`; solver algorithms and requested beam are unchanged.

- Tetraminx integrated private Kaggle v2 completed training, output-1 export, CUDA build, real two-rank launch, and solved all puzzles 990..999. Its worker status was ERROR only because an unrelated original paper-table cell assumed a missing `size` key before submission generation. Updated the notebook generator to skip that optional report and to keep `outputs`/`execution_count` only on code cells; local notebook JSON/schema/AST validation passed and private kernel v3 was pushed. No CUDA/C++ algorithm change.

 # Changelog
 
+- Published the validated CayleyPy results-repository control plane to
+  `TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results` main via PR #1. The public repository now
+  has append-only schema/replay validation, deterministic indexes, serialized
+  staging promotion, and an exact-candidate Check Run contract. Main branch
+  protection requires `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` from the GitHub
+  Actions App (`app_id=15368`), strict up-to-date PRs, and disallows force
+  pushes/deletion. Cloudflare ingest remains disabled until authenticated
+  staging resources, GitHub App secrets, and the 100-publisher recovery gate
+  pass; no beam-search/CUDA architecture was changed.
+
+## 2026-07-22
+- Updated the IHES GitHub-release preparation path to use
+  `p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth` as the default model release asset for
+  `hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`.
+- Added a `model.pth.release_asset` marker check so the default cluster
+  `model.pth` is re-downloaded and Stream1 weights are re-exported when the
+  configured release asset changes, preventing a stale old checkpoint from
+  being reused silently.
+
+## 2026-06-29
+- Added a separate Stream1 transformer export path for Kaggle
+  `piece_transformer` checkpoints. `tools/export_stream1_transformer.py`
+  detects model dimensions from the checkpoint/metadata/generator files,
+  exports the p900 fast input form (`fast_slot_projected` and
+  `fast_piece_static`), transformer block weights, LayerNorm parameters, and a
+  `backend=piece_transformer` manifest without changing the existing MLP
+  exporter.
+- Added `tools/export_stream1.py`, an auto-detecting Stream1 export entrypoint
+  that selects `piece-transformer`, `resmlp-layernorm`<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1987" />s/source_files; requested continuing to collect and publish all cluster IHES results robustly.

def _runtime_env(
    config: PublicRunConfig,
    plan: RuntimePlan,
    weights_dir: Path,
    model: ExportedModel | None = None,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    generator_path = str(config.puzzle_info_json)

        source_generators = model.manifest.get("source_generators")
        if not isinstance(source_generators, str) or not source_generators:
            raise ValueError("piece-transformer export manifest is missing source_generators")
        generator_path = source_generators

        "BEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON": str(config.puzzle_info_json),

        "BEAM_STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS": str(plan.runtime["stream4_active_sort_slots"]),
        "BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY": "0",
        "B<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="617" />
        preflight["model_format"] = model.format
        preflight["model_backend"] = model.backend
        preflight["model_dtype"] = model.dtype
        preflight["output_dim"] = output_dim
        _write_json(output_dir / "preflight.json", preflight)
        backend = model.backend
        runner = locate_or_build_runner(
            output_dir, config.puzzle_info_json, backend=backend, config=config,
        )
        log_redactions = tuple(path for path in (
            arguments.config_json, config.checkpoint_path, config.puzzle_info_json,

  local run_tag="$1"
  local run_log="$2"
  local rank_log_dir="${LOG_DIR}/ranks_${SLURM_JOB_ID:-manual}_${run_tag}"
  local gpu_log="${TUNING_DIR}/nvidia_smi_${run_tag}.log"
  local gpu_monitor_pid=""
  local world_size="${WORLD_SIZE_EFFECTIVE:-${TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE:-8}}"
  mkdir -p "${rank_log_dir}"
  export BEAM_RANK_LOG_DIR="${rank_log_dir}"
  echo "run_tag=${run_tag}"
  echo "run_log=${run_log}"
  echo "rank_log_dir=${BEAM_RANK_LOG_DIR}"
  echo "beam_nccl_id_file=${BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE}"
  echo "gpu_monitor_log=${gpu_log}"
  echo "production_launcher=native"
  echo "production_world_size=${world_size}"
  (
    while true; do
      date -Is
      nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,name,temperature.gpu,power.draw,memory.used,memory.total,utilization.gpu,utilization.memory --format=csv,noheader,nounits
      sleep 5
    done
  ) > "${gpu_log}" 2>&1 &
  gpu_monitor_pid=$!
  set +e
  local pids=()
  local rank
  for ((rank = 0; rank < world_size; rank++)); do
    if [ "${rank}" -eq 0 ]; then
      (
        export RANK=0
        export LOCAL_RANK=0
        export WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        export LOCAL_WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        exec "${BUILD_DIR}/production_runner" "${PUZZLE_ID}" "${DEPTH_LIMIT}" "${BEAM_WIDTH}"
      ) 2>&1 | tee "${run_log}" &
      pids+=("$!")
    else
      (
        export RANK="${rank}"
        export LOCAL_RANK="${rank}"
        export WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        export LOCAL_WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        exec "${BUILD_DIR}/production_runner" "${PUZZLE_ID}" "${DEPTH_LIMIT}" "${BEAM_WIDTH}"
      ) > "${rank_log_dir}/rank${rank}.log" 2>&1 &
      pids+=("$!")
    fi
  done
  local launch_rc=0
  local pid
  for pid in "${pids[@]}"; do
    if ! wait "${pid}"; then
      launch_rc=1
      local other_pid
      for other_pid in "${pids[@]}"; do
        kill "${other_pid}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
      done
    fi
  done
  set -e
  if [ -n "${gpu_monitor_pid}" ]; then
    kill "${gpu_monitor_pid}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    wait "${gpu_monitor_pid}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  fi
  return "${launch_rc}"
}

def _runtime_env(
    config: PublicRunConfig,
    plan: RuntimePlan,
    weights_dir: Path,
    model: ExportedModel | None = None,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    generator_path = str(config.puzzle_info_json)
    if model is not None and model.backend == "piece_transformer":
        source_generators = model.manifest.get("source_generators")
        if not isinstance(source_generators, str) or not source_generators:
            raise ValueError("piece-transformer export manifest is missing source_generators")
        generator_path = source_generators
    env = {
        "BEAM_WEIGHT_DIR": str(weights_dir),
        "BEAM_PUZZLE_INFO_JSON": str(config.puzzle_info_json),
        "BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH": generator_path,

        "BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS": str(plan.runtime["stream3_ring_slots"]),
        "BEAM_SHARD_COUNT": str(plan.runtime["shard_count"]),
        "BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT": "2",
        "BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES": str(plan.shard_capacity_candidates),
        "BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES": str(plan.runtime["stream4_batch_candidates"]),
        "BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES": str(plan.runtime["stream4_trigger_candidates"]),
        "BEAM_STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS": str(plan.runtime["stream4_active_sort_slots"]),
        "BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY": "0",
        "B<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="617" />
        preflight["model_format"] = model.format
        preflight["model_backend"] = model.backend
        preflight["model_dtype"] = model.dtype
        preflight["output_dim"] = output_dim
        _write_json(output_dir / "preflight.json", preflight)
        backend = model.backend
        runner = locate_or_build_runner(
            output_dir, config.puzzle_info_json, backend=backend, config=config,
        )
        log_redactions = tuple(path for path in (
            arguments.config_json, config.checkpoint_path, config.puzzle_info_json,
            config.test_csv, config.sample_submission_csv, config.reflect_source_csv,
            output_dir, export_dir, _REPO_ROOT, Path.home(),

        except PublicSearchRunError as error:
            artifact_summary = _materialize_run_artifacts(error.partial_artifacts, output_dir)
            wall_seconds = time.perf_counter() - started

                config, contract, model, profile, plan, hardware_names,

  local run_tag="$1"
  local run_log="$2"
  local rank_log_dir="${LOG_DIR}/ranks_${SLURM_JOB_ID:-manual}_${run_tag}"
  local gpu_log="${TUNING_DIR}/nvidia_smi_${run_tag}.log"
  local gpu_monitor_pid=""
  local world_size="${WORLD_SIZE_EFFECTIVE:-${TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE:-8}}"
  mkdir -p "${rank_log_dir}"
  export BEAM_RANK_LOG_DIR="${rank_log_dir}"
  echo "run_tag=${run_tag}"
  echo "run_log=${run_log}"
  echo "rank_log_dir=${BEAM_RANK_LOG_DIR}"
  echo "beam_nccl_id_file=${BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE}"
  echo "gpu_monitor_log=${gpu_log}"
  echo "production_launcher=native"
  echo "production_world_size=${world_size}"
  (
    while true; do
      date -Is
      nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,name,temperature.gpu,power.draw,memory.used,memory.total,utilization.gpu,utilization.memory --format=csv,noheader,nounits
      sleep 5
    done
  ) > "${gpu_log}" 2>&1 &
  gpu_monitor_pid=$!
  set +e
  local pids=()
  local rank
  for ((rank = 0; rank < world_size; rank++)); do
    if [ "${rank}" -eq 0 ]; then
      (
        export RANK=0
        export LOCAL_RANK=0
        export WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        export LOCAL_WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        exec "${BUILD_DIR}/production_runner" "${PUZZLE_ID}" "${DEPTH_LIMIT}" "${BEAM_WIDTH}"
      ) 2>&1 | tee "${run_log}" &
      pids+=("$!")
    else
      (
        export RANK="${rank}"
        export LOCAL_RANK="${rank}"
        export WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        export LOCAL_WORLD_SIZE="${world_size}"
        exec "${BUILD_DIR}/production_runner" "${PUZZLE_ID}" "${DEPTH_LIMIT}" "${BEAM_WIDTH}"
      ) > "${rank_log_dir}/rank${rank}.log" 2>&1 &
      pids+=("$!")
    fi
  done
  local launch_rc=0
  local pid
  for pid in "${pids[@]}"; do
    if ! wait "${pid}"; then
      launch_rc=1
      local other_pid
      for other_pid in "${pids[@]}"; do
        kill "${other_pid}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
      done
    fi
  done
  set -e
  if [ -n "${gpu_monitor_pid}" ]; then
    kill "${gpu_monitor_pid}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    wait "${gpu_monitor_pid}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  fi
  return "${launch_rc}"
}

    def kernels_output(
        self, kernel: str, path: str, file_pattern: Optional[str] = None, force: bool = False, quiet: bool = True
    ) -> Tuple[List[str], str]:
        """Retrieves the output for a specified kernel.

        Args:
            kernel (str): The kernel for which to retrieve the output.
            path (str): The path to which to pull the files.
            file_pattern (str): Optional regex pattern to match against filenames. Only files matching the pattern will be downloaded.
            force (bool): If True, force an overwrite if the output already exists (default is False).
            quiet (bool): Suppress verbose output (default is True).

        Returns:
            Tuple[List[str], str]: A tuple containing a list of output files and a string indicating the response status.
        """
        if kernel is None:
            raise ValueError("A kernel must be specified")
        if "/" in kernel:
            self.validate_kernel_string(kernel)
            kernel_url_list = kernel.split("/")
            owner_slug = kernel_url_list[0]
            kernel_slug = kernel_url_list[1]
        else:
            owner_slug = cast(str, self.get_config_value(self.CONFIG_NAME_USER))
            kernel_slug = kernel

        if path is None:
            target_dir = self.get_default_download_dir("kernels", owner_slug, kernel_slug, "output")
        else:
            target_dir = path

        if not os.path.exists(target_dir):
            os.makedirs(target_dir)

        if not os.path.isdir(target_dir):
            raise ValueError("You must specify a directory for the kernels output")

        if file_pattern is not None:
            try:
                compiled_pattern = re.compile(file_pattern)
            except re.error as e:
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid regex pattern '{file<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="62" />rnel_slug
            try:
                response = kaggle.kernels.kernels_api_client.list_kernel_session_output(request)
            except HTTPError as e:
                if e.response.status_code in (401, 403):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Cannot access kernel '{kernel}' (Permission 'kernels.get' was denied). "
                        "The most likely cause is a wrong kernel slug. "
                        "The benchmark_task_slug returned by get_benchmark_leaderboard differs from the actual kernel slug — "
                        "use the slug from the notebook URL (kaggle.com/code/owner/KERNEL-SLUG), not from the leaderboard. "
                        "It can also occur if the notebook is private."
                    )

            token = response.next_page_token

        outfiles = []
        for item in response.files or []:

                continue

            outfile = os.path.join(target_dir, item.file_name)
            outfiles.append(outfile)
            download_response = requests.get(item.url, stream=True)
            if force or self.download_needed(download_response, outfile, quiet):
                os.makedirs(os.path.split(outfile)[0], exist_ok=True)
                with open(outfile, "wb") as out:
                    out.write(download_response.content)
                if not quiet:
                    print("Output file downloaded to %s" % outfile)

        log = response.log
        if log:
            outfile = os.path.join(target_dir, kernel_slug + ".log")
            outfiles.append(outfile)
            with open(outfile, "w") as out:
                out.write(log)
            if not quiet:
                print("Kernel log downloaded to %s " % outfile)

        return outfiles, token  # Breaking change, we need to get the token to the UI

    def kernels_output(
        self, kernel: str, path: str, file_pattern: Optional[str] = None, force: bool = False, quiet: bool = True
    ) -> Tuple[List[str], str]:
        """Retrieves the output for a specified kernel.

        Args:
            kernel (str): The kernel for which to retrieve the output.
            path (str): The path to which to pull the files.
            file_pattern (str): Optional regex pattern to match against filenames. Only files matching the pattern will be downloaded.
            force (bool): If True, force an overwrite if the output already exists (default is False).
            quiet (bool): Suppress verbose output (default is True).

        Returns:
            Tuple[List[str], str]: A tuple containing a list of output files and a string indicating the response status.
        """
        if kernel is None:
            raise ValueError("A kernel must be specified")
        if "/" in kernel:
            self.validate_kernel_string(kernel)
            kernel_url_list = kernel.split("/")
            owner_slug = kernel_url_list[0]
            kernel_slug = kernel_url_list[1]
        else:
            owner_slug = cast(str, self.get_config_value(self.CONFIG_NAME_USER))
            kernel_slug = kernel

        if path is None:
            target_dir = self.get_default_download_dir("kernels", owner_slug, kernel_slug, "output")
        else:
            target_dir = path

        if not os.path.exists(target_dir):
            os.makedirs(target_dir)

        if not os.path.isdir(target_dir):
            raise ValueError("You must specify a directory for the kernels output")

        if file_pattern is not None:
            try:
                compiled_pattern = re.compile(file_pattern)
            except re.error as e:
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid regex pattern '{file<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="62" />rnel_slug
            try:
                response = kaggle.kernels.kernels_api_client.list_kernel_session_output(request)
            except HTTPError as e:
                if e.response.status_code in (401, 403):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"Cannot access kernel '{kernel}' (Permission 'kernels.get' was denied). "
                        "The most likely cause is a wrong kernel slug. "
                        "The benchmark_task_slug returned by get_benchmark_leaderboard differs from the actual kernel slug — "
                        "use the slug from the notebook URL (kaggle.com/code/owner/KERNEL-SLUG), not from the leaderboard. "
                        "It can also occur if the notebook is private."
                    )
                raise
            token = response.next_page_token

        outfiles = []
        for item in response.files or []:
            if compiled_pattern and not compiled_pattern.search(item.file_name):
                continue

            outfile = os.path.join(target_dir, item.file_name)
            outfiles.append(outfile)
            download_response = requests.get(item.url, stream=True)
            if force or self.download_needed(download_response, outfile, quiet):
                os.makedirs(os.path.split(outfile)[0], exist_ok=True)
                with open(outfile, "wb") as out:
                    out.write(download_response.content)
                if not quiet:
                    print("Output file downloaded to %s" % outfile)

        log = response.log
        if log:
            outfile = os.path.join(target_dir, kernel_slug + ".log")
            outfiles.append(outfile)
            with open(outfile, "w") as out:
                out.write(log)
            if not quiet:
                print("Kernel log downloaded to %s " % outfile)

        return outfiles, token  # Breaking change, we need to get the token to the UI
